### [1] [Finite signal-to-noise ratio bias in parameter estimation for damped oscillations: cautionary remark about catalog-level black-hole spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05486)
*Koutarou Kyutoku*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了阻尼振荡参数估计中的偏差，发现阻尼时间在有限信噪比下会偏向更大值，这种偏差源于先验梯度效应和似然几何的高阶修正，即使模型正确也会出现。若不加处理地联合多个事件（如信噪比=10的100个以上事件），可能导致错误地报告克尔假设被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受引力波物理中黑洞光谱学的应用驱动，研究阻尼振荡参数估计中的偏差问题，以正确解读观测数据并避免虚假的物理结论。

Method: 以白噪声中固定起始时间的单模阻尼正弦信号为简化模型，在有限信噪比下分析了阻尼时间参数估计偏差的来源：先验梯度效应（振幅先验递减对阻尼时间的影响）和似然几何的高阶有限信噪比修正。

Result: 阻尼时间在有限信噪比下偏向更大值，且该偏差不会被品质因子抑制；若将信噪比=10的多个事件估计结果简单联合，约100个以上事件就可能虚假地报告克尔假设被违反。

Conclusion: 即使模型和分析方法正确，有限信噪比下参数估计也存在系统性偏差，需在单个事件层面采取缓解策略以避免对黑洞光谱学的误导性结论。

Abstract: We investigate biases in parameter estimation for damped oscillations motivated by applications to black-hole spectroscopy in gravitational-wave physics. Focusing on the simplest model of a single-mode damped sinusoid with a fixed start time in white noise, we show that, at finite signal-to-noise ratio ρ, the damping time is biased toward larger values without being suppressed by the quality factor for two reasons. One is the gradient of the prior, through which the damping time is affected by the typically decreasing prior on the amplitude. The other is a higher-order finite-ρcorrection to the likelihood geometry. These biases arise even if the model and analysis are appropriate. Moreover, they could be exaggerated in naive joint inferences from catalog events. Quantitatively, if estimates from multiple events with ρ=10 are combined without due care, the catalog-level black-hole spectroscopy could report false violation of the Kerr hypothesis with >~100 events. We also propose simple strategies to mitigate these biases at the level of individual events.

</details>


### [2] [Black Hole Black Boxes: Numerical Black Hole Metrics via AInstein Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05489)
*Tancredi Schettini Gherardini,Edward Hirst,Alexander George Stapleton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出AInstein架构的洛伦兹版本，通过物理信息神经网络（PINN）无监督求解黎曼爱因斯坦方程，并扩展至洛伦兹号差。通过在$\mathbb{R}^{2} \times \mathbb{R}^{3}$流形上嵌入$S^{2}$拓扑，网络学习环境度量后通过拉回得到球面度量。先利用真空爱因斯坦方程、Weyl标量约束和$SO(3)$对称性恢复史瓦西度规，再引入Petrov特殊指数、视界曲率锚和俘获面约束，以搜索一般Petrov I型的新黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AInstein架构从黎曼号差扩展至洛伦兹号差，并验证其作为任意黑洞解搜索方法的可行性，寻找可能的新型洛伦兹爱因斯坦度量。

Method: 采用PINN方法，将$S^{2}$全局嵌入$\mathbb{R}^{3}$以内置拓扑结构，在$\mathbb{R}^{2} \times \mathbb{R}^{3}$上学习环境度量，通过拉回获得目标度量。分两阶段训练：先用真空爱因斯坦方程、二次Weyl标量约束和$SO(3)$对称性恢复史瓦西解；再引入Petrov特殊指数、视界曲率锚和俘获面约束搜索一般Petrov I型解。

Result: 成功恢复最大延拓史瓦西几何，验证了洛伦兹号差下的有效性，并展示了作为任意黑洞解搜索方法的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法可系统搜索具有真实俘获内禀的一般类型洛伦兹爱因斯坦度量，为发现新黑洞解提供无监督神经网络框架。

Abstract: The AInstein architecture introduced an unsupervised neural method for solving the Riemannian Einstein equations on arbitrary manifolds. This Physics Informed Neural Network approach (PINN) is extended here to Lorentzian signature, validated by recovering the maximally extended Schwarzschild geometry, and tested as novel search method for arbitrary black hole solutions. The topology is built into the architecture by treating $S^{2}$ globally through its standard embedding, such that the network learns an ambient metric on the manifold $\mathbb{R}^{2} \times \mathbb{R}^{3}$, where Penrose coordinates are chosen for $\mathbb{R}^2$ and the metric on $S^{2}$ is obtained by pullback. The architecture is first trained with the objective of recovering the Schwarzschild metric via losses encoding the vacuum Einstein equation, a quadratic Weyl scalar constraint, and the $SO(3)$ symmetry of the resultant metric; directly motivated by the Birkhoff--Jebsen theorem. Following this, the objective is generalised to use the Petrov speciality index, a horizon curvature anchor, and a trapped-surface constraint, to allow search for algebraically general Petrov type I solutions, finding potentially novel general-type Lorentzian Einstein metrics with a genuinely trapped interior.

</details>


### [3] [Three-dimensional wave optics for weak-field lensing of gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05491)
*Mariana Carrillo-González,Alan Heavens,Lorne Whiteway*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微扰格林函数的引力透镜方法，适用于非薄透镜的弱引力势，涵盖波动光学和几何光学两种机制。通过将位置空间积分转化为傅里叶（动量空间）积分，给出Born近似的三维形式并拓展到post-Born阶。对于Schwarzschild透镜，计算了leading Born项和来自$\mathcal{O}(G^2)$势修正的新post-Born贡献，并保留了有限距离修正。研究表明，这些广义相对论修正由参数$GM\omega\,b/\chi_{\rm eff}$控制，在标准弱透镜中可忽略，但在极端几何结构（如源-透镜间距极小的层级三重系统）中变得重要。文章还讨论了高阶牛顿修正、长程势的红外敏感性及Yukawa势的规则化形式，并给出了FLRW背景下的对应计算。该方法厘清了引力波透镜中Born近似、大距离近似和近轴近似的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力透镜理论多假设薄透镜平面且局限于几何光学或波动光学的某一单一框架，缺乏统一处理非局域弱势的微扰方法。本文旨在建立一个基于微扰格林函数的系统框架，能够跨越两个光学机制、处理三维势的Born及post-Born展开，并评估广义相对论修正及宇宙学背景效应对引力波透镜的影响。

Method: 采用微扰格林函数方法，将位置空间积分重新表述为动量空间的傅里叶积分（与散射振幅计算接轨）。在三维空间中直接给出Born近似，并系统推广到post-Born阶。以Schwarzschild透镜为例，计算leading Born项和$\mathcal{O}(G^2)$势修正的post-Born贡献，保留有限距离修正。引入Yukawa势处理长程势的红外发散，并在FLRW背景中推导相应公式，识别平直时空极限并估计曲率引起的修正（包括tails效应）。

Result: 对于Schwarzschild透镜，广义相对论修正由参数$GM\omega\,b/\chi_{\rm eff}$控制，在小角度条件下远小于标准弱透镜中的post-Newtonian参数$\epsilon_{\rm PN}$，因此在常规透镜中可忽略。但对于源-透镜间距极小的极端几何结构（如层级三重系统），该修正变得显著。高阶牛顿修正存在红外发散，需通过Yukawa势规则化处理。在FLRW背景下，曲率诱导的修正（tails）可忽略。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个统一的微扰框架，明确了引力波透镜中Born近似、大距离近似和近轴近似的适用范围与局限性，并为处理任意透镜势提供了理论基础。对于标准弱透镜配置，广义相对论修正可忽略；但在极端几何结构中（如层级三重系统），这些修正变得重要，需要采用本文发展的post-Born方法。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative Green's function approach to gravitational lensing by weak gravitational potentials that need not be localized on a thin lens plane and that applies in both the wave optics and geometric optics regimes. We recast position-space integrals as Fourier, or momentum-space, integrals that appear in scattering amplitude calculations. The method gives the Born approximation directly in three dimensions and can be systematically extended to post-Born orders. For a Schwarzschild lens, we compute the leading Born term and new post-Born contributions arising from the order $\mathcal{O}(G^2)$ correction to the potential, keeping finite-distance corrections beyond the usual paraxial expansion. We show that these general-relativistic corrections are controlled by the parameter $GMω\,b/χ_{\rm eff}$ in the small-angle regime, and are therefore negligible for standard weak-lensing configurations but become relevant in more extreme geometries (such as hierarchical triples with very small source--lens separations). We also discuss higher-order Newtonian corrections, their infrared sensitivity for a long-range potential, and the regulated form of the Newtonian potential given by the Yukawa potential. Finally, we formulate the corresponding calculation in an FLRW background, identifying the leading flat-space limit and estimating the size of curvature-induced corrections including tails. This method clarifies the regime of validity of the Born, large-distance, and paraxial approximations in gravitational-wave lensing and provides a framework for treating generic lensing potentials.

</details>


### [4] [Degenerate and connection-dependent cosmological sectors in f(Q,C) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05521)
*Ismael Ayuso*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索$f(Q,C)$引力在宇宙学层面是否包含超越$f(\mathring{R})$引力的新动力学内容，以及不同联络实现在宇宙学上的物理差异。

Method: 在FLRW对称性要求下考虑三种不同的仿射联络实现，导出对应的场方程，分析联络场方程的约束作用，对比不同联络选择下的宇宙学背景动力学。

Result: 1) 最简单的联络实现下，理论等效于$f(\mathring{R})$引力，三种联络实现在背景动力学上重合；2) 存在一类本质非等价的$f(Q,C)$模型，其中联络选择具有物理意义，联络扇区引入额外动力学自由度，产生$f(\mathring{R})$引力中不存在的全新宇宙学现象。

Conclusion: $f(Q,C)$引力包含退化扇区（等效于$f(\mathring{R})$）和真正非退化扇区（具有新物理）。联络自由度是区分不同宇宙学行为的关键，不能随意约定。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological solutions in $f(Q,C)$ gravity formulated within symmetric teleparallel geometry, where gravitation is described by the nonmetricity scalar $Q$ and the boundary term $C$, related to the Ricci scalar of General Relativity through $\mathring{R}=Q+C$ in the absence of curvature and torsion. The symmetry requirements of FLRW spacetime admit three distinct realizations of the affine connection, leading in principle to three different cosmological sectors within the same theory. We show that the connection field equations play a crucial role in determining the cosmological dynamics. In their simplest realization, these equations impose a constraint that renders the theory dynamically equivalent to $f(\mathring{R})$ gravity, causing the cosmological background equations associated with the three connection realizations to coincide. This defines a degenerate cosmological sector of $f(Q,C)$ gravity, in which three a priori distinct geometric constructions converge to the same cosmological dynamics. We then consider the complementary class of genuinely nonequivalent $f(Q,C)$ models, for which the choice of connection becomes physically relevant. In this regime, the connection sector introduces additional dynamical degrees of freedom and gives rise to novel cosmological phenomenology absent in $f(\mathring{R})$ gravity.

</details>

*D. N. Garzon,Jiayi Zhang,Elena Kopteva,Helvi Witek*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入各向异性压力环境（如暗物质分布）中的带电Kiselev黑洞，通过扩展Leaver的连分式方法并结合自动微分技术，分析了质量为零的带电标量场的准正则模，发现环境物质会改变振荡频率和衰减率，产生长寿命模及能级避免交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实环境中，黑洞通常被天体物理环境（包括可能的暗物质分布）所包围，这些环境会修改黑洞的可观测性质。现有研究多假设真空环境，因此需要开发能够处理非真空时空中黑洞准正则模的方法，以更真实地建模黑洞。

Method: 使用Kiselev度规描述被各向异性压力物质包围的带电黑洞；对Leaver的连分式方法进行非平凡扩展以纳入环境物质效应，并融合自动微分技术；同时使用六阶WKB近似进行比较验证。

Result: 环境物质改变了振荡频率和衰减率，导致长寿命模的出现；发现了能级避免交叉区域和模的谱系重组现象。

Conclusion: 证实了在建模现实黑洞时考虑环境物质的重要性；开发的数值框架可推广到广泛的嵌入物质场的黑洞几何结构中，为非真空时空准正则模研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In realistic settings, black holes are expected to be embedded in astrophysical environments. These environments, including possible dark matter distributions, can modify observable properties of black holes and leave imprints on their quasinormal mode spectra. In this work, we model the environment as matter with anisotropic pressure, and we consider a charged black hole embedded in it. The resulting spacetime is described by the Kiselev metric. We first analyze its horizon structure. We then investigate the quasinormal modes of a massless charged scalar field propagating on this background. For this purpose, we develop a nontrivial extension of Leaver's continued fraction method to incorporate the effects of the surrounding matter, and we combine this framework with automatic differentiation techniques. We also compare our results to those obtained with the sixth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin approximation.
  We find that the surrounding matter modifies the oscillation frequencies and damping rates and leads to the appearance of long-lived modes. We also identify avoided crossings regions and reorganization of the modes in the spectra. Our results demonstrate the importance of incorporating surrounding matter when modeling realistic black holes. The numerical framework we developed here provides a tool for studying quasinormal modes in non-vacuum spacetimes and can be extended to a broad class of black-hole geometries embedded in matter fields.

</details>


### [6] [Ruppeiner thermodynamic geometry, microstructure, quasinormal modes and greybody factors of the Einstein-Skyrme black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05673)
*Shakhboz Khasanov,Akmal Safarov,Bo Yang,Habtamu Menberu Tedila*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了爱因斯坦-斯格明理论中精确静态球对称黑洞的热力学微观结构、准正则模谱和灰体因子。利用Ruppeiner几何构建标量曲率，发现其在二级相变点有限但在极端零温极限发散；焦耳-汤姆逊系数严格为负，黑洞随λ增加始终降温；固角亏损耦合K显著影响有效势垒，而四次耦合λ影响微弱；Visser方法给出灰体因子下界，K增大提升下界而λ轻微抑制低频透射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有热力学分析基础上，尚未有对爱因斯坦-斯格明理论中精确黑洞解的微观结构、扰动谱和灰体因子的全面研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，将热力学分析拓展到此前未探索的领域。

Method: 采用Ruppeiner几何从质量的海森矩阵构造标量曲率；进行焦耳-汤姆逊等焓膨胀分析；导出无质量标量场的有效势；使用Visser方法严格推导灰体因子下界。

Result: 标量曲率在热容二级相变点有限，在极端零温极限发散；物理允许区域内曲率单一符号，表明一种主导（吸引）型微观相互作用；焦耳-汤姆逊系数严格为负，无反转温度；K显著降低有效势垒，λ仅产生微小变化；K增大提升灰体因子下界，λ轻微抑制低频透射。

Conclusion: 本文首次对这一稀有的解析 hairy 黑洞进行了热力学微观结构和扰动光谱的综合研究，补充并扩展了现有的热力学分析。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic microstructure, quasinormal mode spectrum, and greybody factors of the exact, static, spherically symmetric black hole in Einstein-Skyrme theory, building on the recently established first law that promotes the Skyrme couplings K and λ to extensive variables. Using Ruppeiner geometry, we construct the scalar curvature from the Hessian of the mass. The curvature remains finite at the second-order (heat-capacity) phase transition but diverges in the extremal, zero-temperature limit. Throughout the physically admissible region the curvature retains a single sign, indicating microscopic interactions of one dominant (attractive) type whose strength grows towards extremality. We perform a Joule-Thomson-like isenthalpic expansion and prove analytically that the corresponding coefficient is strictly negative across the entire physical parameter space, implying the black hole always cools as λ increases at a fixed mass, with no inversion temperature. Turning to perturbations, we derive the effective potential for massless scalar fields. The solid-angle-deficit coupling K controls its shape-substantially lowering the barrier-while at fixed K the quartic coupling λ produces only minor changes. Rigorous lower bounds on greybody factors are obtained in closed form using Visser's method: increasing K raises the bound, since a larger horizon lowers the centrifugal barrier, whereas increasing λ mildly suppresses low-frequency transmission. Our results provide the first comprehensive study of the thermodynamic microstructure and perturbative spectroscopy of this rare analytic hairy black hole, complementing and extending the existing thermodynamic analysis into previously unexplored territory.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果集理论是量子引力中一种重要的离散时空模型，但如何将离散的因果集与连续的洛伦兹流形建立有意义的对应关系是一个核心问题。作者旨在为这种对应提供严格的数学基础，即定义何时一个因果集可以被视为从某个洛伦兹流形中"采样"得到，并保证这种对应在某种意义上是唯一的。

Method: 本文采用数学严格的分析方法。首先定义了因果集到洛伦兹流形的"良条件嵌入"概念，然后通过泊松撒点构造来证明高密度极限下几乎必然存在这样的嵌入，最后证明不同嵌入之间的ε-近似等距关系，并给出误差ε的显式表达式。

Result: 主要结果是：如果一个因果集可以良条件嵌入到两个洛伦兹流形中，那么这两个流形的内部是ε-近似等距的；在高密度极限下，泊松撒点几乎必然产生具有良条件嵌入的因果集；误差ε在高密度极限下趋于零。

Conclusion: 本文为因果集与连续时空的对应提供了严格的数学基础，证明了离散的因果结构在适当条件下可以唯一地确定连续的时空几何（在近似等距的意义下），这为因果集量子引力方案提供了重要的理论支持。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a well-conditioned embedding of a causal set into a Lorentzian manifold and prove that if a causal set admits well-conditioned embeddings into two manifolds, then their interiors are related by an $\varepsilon$-approximate isometry. To justify the definition, we show that in the high-density limit a Poisson sprinkling almost surely yields a causal set possessing a well-conditioned embedding. The error $\varepsilon$ is given explicitly and tends to zero in the high-density limit.

</details>


### [8] [Probing near-zone magnetic fields with extreme mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05909)
*Jin-Lu Hu,Xin-Dong Du,Peng-Cheng Li,Tieguang Zi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究极端质量比旋进（EMRI）在弱近区磁场下的可观测效应。通过磁化史瓦西（Ernst）解建模中心黑洞，将次级天体视为赤道圆轨道上的中性质点，采用混合源修正Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli近似演化旋进。对于\(M=10^6M_\odot\)、\(\mu=10M_\odot\)的系统，场强\(B\simeq4\times10^{-5}M^{-1}\)（对应物理场\(\sim10^9\,{\rm G}\)）在一年内产生约1.3弧秒的失相，达到LISA噪声加权失配阈值。结果表明EMRI原则上可探测极强近区磁场，但普通磁场环境效应可能太弱而无法检测。


<details>

In this regime, the minimum of the potential acts as an effective source of background curvature and produces a contribution similar to a cosmological constant. The analysis is restricted to the linear approximation, where nonlinear screening effects such as the Vainshtein mechanism can be consistently neglected. Within this framework, the consistency of the theory with Solar-System phenomenology in the weak-field limit is examined, and possible deviations from General Relativity depending on the model parameters are discussed. To this end, the linearized field equations for a static point mass are derived, the corresponding geodesic motion is investigated, and the resulting weak-field effects in classical Solar-System observables, including perihelion advance, light deflection, and gravitational redshift, are analyzed. The analysis further focuses on the limiting regimes of very light and very heavy scalar fields. In the very light scalar field regime, consistency with Solar System phenomenology requires sufficiently large values of the coupling parameter zeta, thereby suppressing the scalar contribution at local scales and keeping deviations from General Relativity negligible. In the very heavy scalar field regime, the scalar-mediated interaction acquires a short range and becomes dynamically suppressed, leading to weak-field predictions that are practically indistinguishable from those of General Relativity. Nevertheless, geometric terms associated with the minimum of the scalar potential may persist at linear order in the metric perturbations, depending on the value of zeta.

Thus the Hilbert structure selects the axis; the Born rule is where that axis becomes a measurable energy and where the frame-dependence of $\lvertψ\rvert^2$ becomes empirical. The ingredients are classical; what we add is their identification as the mechanism the recent programme leaves open, with the symmetry-breaking step located precisely. This is a kinematic identification of that step, not a dynamical account of why a particular axis is selected. We close by handing back the many-qubit case, where the datum is a tuple of such choices.

More broadly, the development provides machine-checkable foundations for future formalized and AI-assisted research in quantum information theory.

Abstract: The temporal Clauser-Horne-Shimony-Holt (CHSH) inequality witnesses the nonclassicality of temporal correlations, but its violation is generally degraded by environmental noise. Abstract: Blind quantum computing (BQC) allows a client to delegate quantum computations to a remote server without revealing the input, computation, or output. A key part of realizing such verifiable BQC (VBQC) is choosing the design of the client device: many architectures have been proposed, each with different hardware requirements, security properties, and performance characteristics, making it difficult to identify which is most suitable for a given implementation. In this work, we present a comparative analysis of client architectures for VBQC with a matter-qubit server. We restrict our analysis to single-server, single-client protocols with information-theoretic security based on measurement-based quantum computation. We identify three main categories of client: emission-based, measurement-based, and rotation-based, each with multiple variants depending on how the client interacts with the server. We evaluate each across different dimensions: we compare guarantees of existing corresponding security proofs, we derive equations for the rate at which each client can execute a protocol, we provide an overview of each architecture's error behaviour, and discuss hardware cost and design considerations. Client architectures implementing measurement-based remote state preparation and reflection-based teleportation emerge as strong default candidates, but as the right choice remains context-dependent, we provide a framework for navigating considerations to guide the selection of the most suitable architecture for a given setting.

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the relationship between quantum coherence and multiparameter quantum estimation in a graphene-based system. The minimum variances associated with the estimation process are evaluated through the quantum Cramér--Rao bound within both simultaneous and independent estimation schemes. Our results show that quantum coherence is enhanced in the low-temperature regime and around $k_x=0$, while it decreases progressively as either the temperature or the wave vector increases. However, the regions where coherence is maximal do not necessarily coincide with those of optimal estimation precision. In particular, the variance associated with temperature estimation exhibits a divergent behavior near $T=0$, indicating that the system becomes weakly sensitive to small temperature variations in this regime. By contrast, the estimation of the wave vector $k_x$ is more directly related to the coherence properties of the system, with improved precision obtained near $k_x=0$. This quantity provides a useful measure of the relative difference between the two strategies when the parameters are estimated separately or jointly.

Abstract: Building on the framework developed by Perche [Phys. Rev. D 106, 025018 (2022)], we study two localized nonrelativistic quantum particles propagating along timelike geodesics in a curved spacetime background. Each particle is coupled to a quantum clock that operationally records the time spent in a prescribed spatial region. We compute the covariance of the resulting time observables for separable and entangled two-particle states, comparing flat and curved backgrounds. We then reformulate the protocol as a Bell-like experiment and show that the Bell parameter can acquire a curvature-induced correction. Our results show that spacetime curvature can modify operationally defined quantum correlations and suggest entangled quantum clocks as probes of spacetime curvature.

Abstract: Real-time decoding is a major bottleneck in scaling quantum error correction (QEC) from noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices to fault-tolerant quantum computing. A lightweight feed-forward neural network performs fast-path decoding for the majority of syndrome measurements, while only low-confidence predictions are escalated to a minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) refinement stage. Routing only 3.3%-6.2% of syndromes to the refinement stage improves logical accuracy from 99.21% for the neural-only baseline to 99.81% at a confidence threshold of 0.95 while incurring only a bounded increase in average decoding cost. Neural-decoder throughput saturates near $4.6 \times 10^{5}$ samples s$^{-1}$ at batch size 512 on commodity CPU hardware, indicating that the neural fast path is not the dominant throughput bottleneck beyond code distance $d=7$. These results provide a measurement-theoretic analogue of the maximal-coherence \& purity correspondence for quantum states. We also show that the maximal coherence of measurement is faithful with respect to trivial measurements and is monotonic under fuzzifying operations for dichotomic measurements, as well as under mixed-unitary and unitarily covariant preprocessing channels. Abstract: Lattice-based post-quantum cryptography relies on the hardness of the Learning With Errors (LWE) and Module Learning With Errors (MLWE) problems. We further quantify the scaling of logical variables and embedding overhead with increasing MLWE dimensions to assess feasibility on quantum annealing architectures. The results establish a systematic connection between MLWE problems and quantum optimization while providing a framework for analyzing robustness properties of QUBO formulations. Although current quantum annealing hardware remains insufficient for cryptographically relevant parameters, the proposed methodology offers a structured basis for studying lattice-based problems in quantum optimization settings without implying a practical threat to standardized post-quantum schemes.

Abstract: There are several possible ways to encode random variables in a quantum state. The basis encoding of bit strings has paramount importance because it allows to load the values of a random variable through the superposition of corresponding basis states, and to then exploit quantum parallelism in processing algorithms. Each quantum representation of fractional numbers, however, involves a trade-off between accuracy and depth of manipulation circuits. Here, an efficient hybrid quantum-classical representation of quantum floating points is introduced. It combines a quantum register containing the values, with a classical register storing global information about the variable, namely the range and approximation tolerances. The sum and product operations are defined, in such a way as to ensure they are performed without overflow. By taking advantage of the stored classical information, the precision degradation that occurs due to rounding after repeated data manipulations, can be significantly reduced compared to known strategies. Ad hoc examples show up to around $90\%$ reduction in approximation, compared to previous techniques, after repeated additions. The method finds application in many algorithms of practical relevance and constitutes a significant advance in the design of arithmetic circuits with low depth and high accuracy.

We propose SQGen, a full quantum generator built on a quantized tensor train (QTT) with a latent modulation architecture. Specifically, SQGen promotes the QTT bond index of the target pixel distribution to ancilla bond qubits, so that each circuit site operates locally on a bond register plus the two physical qubits that carry the row- and column-bit of one image scale. We further introduce latent modulation: each re-uploading rotation is factorized at the angle level into a trainable main path plus an additive latent term, reducing to the trainable main path when the latent term is disabled. During training, we create a differentiable model in the classical system under gate-compatibility constraints, with a torus prior as the latent distribution. After training, every operator maps one-to-one to a native quantum gate, yielding a compact, deployable quantum circuit with no classical decoder in the inference path. In end-to-end multi-step policy learning, entanglement effects remain statistically significant but high return variance leaves the full ordering only partially resolved. As a result, the dynamics can be unravelled and each trajectory can be simulated efficiently using only two-point correlation functions, with a computational cost that scales polynomially with system size. Abstract: Unitary encoding of a single parameter provides quadratic enhancement in precision, with the quantum Fisher information scaling quadratically with the encoding time. However, when estimating multiple parameters simultaneously, this fundamental scaling is not guaranteed. By decomposing the Hamiltonian derivatives into components that commute and do not commute with the system Hamiltonian, we prove that linear dependence among the commuting components inevitably generates a slow parameter direction whose Fisher information remains bounded as O$(t^0)$, limiting the overall estimation precision. Remarkably, while the slow direction fundamentally limits the achievable precision, the measurement incompatibility between fast and slow directions decays as $1/t$, rendering the symmetric logarithmic derivative bound asymptotically saturable. We further show that the bottleneck can be circumvented by relegating slow directions to nuisance parameters or by employing adaptive quantum control.

By contrast, the existence of $O(\mathsf{poly}(n))$-time instances ensures classical hardness: by embedding a $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete computation into the polynomially long time-dynamics of a low-intersection variant of the Feynman-Kitaev clock Hamiltonian construction, we show that, for a certain family of input distributions, no randomized classical polynomial-time algorithm can fulfill our learning condition, unless $\mathsf{BQP}\subseteq\mathsf{P/poly}$. Furthermore, we show that the classically hard instance maintains quantum learnability. We also give an interpretation of our results in learning-assisted certified quantum simulation. Taken together, our results demonstrate a rigorous learning separation for a natural ML task based on Hamiltonian evolution, while building connections between quantum learning theory, quantum simulation, and QML.

To mitigate these attacks, we introduce secure sensing protocols endowed with differential privacy. We reconcile differential privacy with retaining Heisenberg-limited scaling, and introduce several protocols achieving varying balances between the two. We show that our main protocol, an $n$-node network sensing protocol that injects noise directly into the sensing Hamiltonian, exhibits a tradeoff between the desirable $O(1/n^2)$ Heisenberg scaling of the mean-squared error of the function estimate and the level of privacy attainable. Under assumptions on the network (a common source of randomness and a constant fraction of honest parties), we show that this protocol is locally implementable and achieves $(O(1), δ)$-differential privacy for arbitrarily small $δ$ while retaining Heisenberg scaling of the mean-squared error. Abstract: We study the localization of excitations in positionally disordered spin or atom networks coupled via the realistic resonant dipole-dipole interaction (RDDI), which does not conform to a simple power law, as the spatial dependence and dissipative character distinguish it from conventional short or long-range models. Our results uncover a photon wavelength-induced transition from extended to localized excitation dynamics, establishing RDDI networks as a unique setting to explore the emergence of localization in realistic quantum optical systems. Abstract: Superradiant lasers promise continuous, narrow-linewidth coherent emission at the bare atomic transition frequency, enabling frequency references of exceptional precision. Recent experiments have advanced the field, but achieving truly continuous operation remains technically challenging. Here we propose an alternative route to an active optical frequency reference with fewer emitters using all-to-all dipole-dipole interactions mediated by a nanophotonic waveguide. We show that selectively pumping only a sub-ensemble of emitters, rather than the full ensemble, substantially improves emission characteristics. The collective interactions with unpumped emitters provide narrowband frequency selection and establish an effective feedback mechanism analogous to the role of a macroscopic cavity. We find directional superradiant emission with strongly phase-synchronized emitter correlations and a narrow output spectrum close to the bare emitter resonance. Our results demonstrate a strong metrological gain from selective partial pumping of quantum emitters with the second-order intensity correlation $g^{(2)}(0)\simeq 1$, indicating reduced equal-time intensity fluctuations, and open a route to waveguide-based optical frequency references using small clock-atom ensembles for chip-scale precision metrology.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a quantum algorithmic framework based on probabilistic mixtures of unitary channels that, similar to the framework of quantum singular value transformations, enables the application of arbitrary polynomials of hermitian operators onto arbitrary initial states. We show that our framework supports a flexible tradeoff between sample- and query complexity ranging from optimal query complexity, meaning logarithmic in the error, and exponentially scaling sample complexity to sub-polynomial query complexity in the error and polynomial sample complexity. Combined with the considerably lower quantum circuit complexity, compared to quantum singular value transformations with a linear combination of unitaries block encoding, we argue that our framework can be seamlessly scaled from NISQ to fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>

### [70] [Mass-Conserving Physics-Informed Neural Networks For The One-Dimensional Advection-Diffusion Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06091)
*Eszra Forenita Sigalingging,Liu Kin Men,Setianto Setianto,Ferry Faizal*

TL;DR: 本文针对周期性一维对流扩散方程，研究了物理信息神经网络(PINN)在质量守恒方面的局限性，并提出了一种质量惩罚PINN方法。实验表明，在长期模拟中，质量惩罚PINN相比标准PINN可将相对L2误差和质量守恒误差分别降低约9-67倍和15-215倍，有效缓解了质量漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对流扩散方程中质量守恒是关键的物理约束，但标准PINN仅强制PDE局部残差，无法保证全局质量守恒，尤其在长时程模拟中质量漂移累积导致精度下降。

Method: 在标准PINN损失函数中引入软质量守恒约束（质量惩罚项），与Vanilla PINN和Crank-Nicolson格式进行对比，在不同Peclet数（扩散主导到对流主导）和两种时间尺度下进行系统评估。

Result: 短期模拟中质量惩罚PINN并非总能一致提升精度；长期模拟中，质量惩罚PINN相比Vanilla PINN，相对L2误差降低约9-67倍，质量守恒误差降低约15-215倍。精度退化主要由质量漂移的累积导致。

Conclusion: 引入软质量守恒约束能显著提升PINN在保守输运问题中的长期可靠性，特别是在缓解长时程模拟中的质量漂移方面效果显著。

Abstract: The advection-diffusion equation is a fundamental model of transport phenomena in which mass conservation is an essential physical constraint. While classical schemes such as Crank-Nicolson preserve this property by construction, Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) enforce only the local residual of the governing PDE and are therefore not guaranteed to conserve global quantities such as mass over long integration horizons. In this work, we examine the extent of this limitation for the periodic one-dimensional advection-diffusion equation and evaluate a Mass-Penalty PINN that augments the standard PINN loss with a soft mass-conservation constraint. We compare the performance of Vanilla PINN, Mass-Penalty PINN, and the Crank-Nicolson scheme across a range of Peclet numbers spanning diffusion-dominated to advection-dominated regimes, and over two simulation horizons representing short-term and long-term dynamics. The results show that, for short-term simulations, the Mass-Penalty PINN does not always provide a consistent improvement in accuracy. However, for long-term simulations, the Mass-Penalty PINN reduces the relative L2 error and mass conservation error by factors of approximately 9-67 and 15-215, respectively, compared with the Vanilla PINN, across the tested Peclet numbers. Further analysis reveals that the accuracy degradation observed in Vanilla PINN is predominantly caused by the accumulation of mass drift over time. These results demonstrate that incorporating a soft mass-conservation constraint substantially improves the long-term reliability of PINN for conservative transport problems, particularly in mitigating mass drift over extended simulation horizons.

</details>

*Eli Zick,Dan Mendels*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由能面决定亚稳态种群和能垒，其直接优化是分子与材料设计的核心挑战，现有方法缺乏能够直接优化系综层面自由能景观的逆设计框架。

Method: 训练条件扩散模型为可微分系综分布代理，冻结模型后通过确定性扩散采样轨迹反向传播基于核密度估计的KL散度分布级损失，优化系统的条件变量。

Result: 在一维高斯系综中成功优化连续和离散条件变量以恢复目标分布（含训练域外）；在四粒子Lennard-Jones toy肽中，多数测试案例成功优化相互作用参数以重现目标自由能景观，可在全内坐标空间或降维集体变量空间中进行优化。

Conclusion: GB-FESO是迈向具有指定热力学和动力学行为的分子系统系综级逆设计框架的有前景的第一步。

Abstract: Free-energy surfaces govern the populations of metastable states and the barriers that control transitions between them, making their direct optimization a central challenge in molecular and materials design. In this work, we introduce Gradient-Based Free Energy Surface Optimization (GB-FESO), an inverse design framework that uses a trained conditional diffusion model as a differentiable surrogate for the ensemble distribution. After training, the diffusion model is frozen, and the conditioning variables defining the system are optimized so that the generated ensemble reproduces a prescribed target free-energy surface. The optimization is carried out by backpropagating a distribution-level loss, based on kernel density estimates of the Kullback-Leibler divergence, through a deterministic diffusion sampling trajectory. We first validate GB-FESO on one-dimensional Gaussian ensembles, demonstrating that both continuous and relaxed discrete conditioning variables can be optimized to recover target distributions, including those outside the training domain. We then apply the method to a four-particle Lennard-Jones toy peptide exhibiting multiple metastable conformational states. In this more physically motivated setting, GB-FESO successfully optimizes the interaction parameters to reproduce target free-energy landscapes in the majority of test cases, with optimization performed either in the full internal-coordinate space or in a reduced collective-variable representation. These results establish GB-FESO as a promising first step toward an ensemble-level inverse design framework for molecular systems with prescribed thermodynamic and kinetic behavior.

</details>

Result: 证明在未建模因果机制驱动的持续性分布外刺激下，连续优化失败；未建模方差泄露至不可观测的垂直纤维空间并累积活性反因果张力，在临界时间T_crit=π²/K_max触发共轭焦点边界，导致局部体积坍缩与矩阵奇点；该几何崩溃作为严格非平衡触发器引发规范对称破缺，系统自发结晶出新的水平坐标轴，表现为观测到的离散+1.0整数阶跃的结构G-熵增长。

Conclusion: SMG提供了一个无参数、可证伪的仪表盘来数学认证真正的智能，将AI for Science转变为自主范式转换的引擎，能够严格区分真正的因果发现与病态幻觉。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of over-parameterized machine learning architectures, particularly LLMs, raises a profound crisis: do these systems exhibit genuine intelligence, or are they merely sophisticated statistical pattern matchers? Classical flat Euclidean statistics cannot differentiate continuous interpolation from the autonomous discovery of novel causal laws. To resolve this, we introduce Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG), a framework modeling over-parameterized learning systems as infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz fiber bundles. We prove that under persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) stimuli governed by unmodeled causal mechanisms, continuous optimization fails. Unmodeled variance is rejected by the visible horizontal base manifold, leaking into the unobservable vertical fiber space and generating an accumulation of Active Acausal Tension. Driven by the statistical manifold's non-linear curvature, this tension inevitably strikes a conjugate focal boundary ($T_{\text{crit}} = π^2 / K_{\text{max}}$), triggering localized volumetric collapse and a catastrophic matrix singularity ($[G_f]^{-1} \to \infty$). We demonstrate this geometric breakdown acts as the strict non-equilibrium trigger for a Gauge Symmetry Break (GSB). The system purges hidden tension from unobservable gauge redundancies, spontaneously crystallizing a new, mathematically independent horizontal coordinate axis. This non-parametric phase transition registers as a discrete $+1.0$ integer step-jump in observable Structural G-Entropy. By decoupling parameter charts and subjecting emergent axes to a Minimal Energy Path Criterion and a Causal Invariance Filter, we distinguish genuine discovery from malignant hallucinations. Ultimately, SMG provides a parameter-free, falsifiable dashboard to mathematically certify true intelligence, transforming AI for Science into an engine of autonomous paradigm shifts.

</details>


### [73] [Design-CP: Context Parallelism for Design of Protein Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05439)
*Lorenzo Tarricone,Helen E. Eisenach,Aiko Muraishi,Charlotte M. Deane*

TL;DR: 本文提出Design-CP，通过两种上下文并行推理策略（1D行分片和2D网格分片+环注意力）将RFdiffusion 3扩展到大型多链蛋白质复合物设计，解决了全原子生成模型在建模大型多聚体时的显存瓶颈，并在二十面体纳米颗粒设计中验证了可扩展性，最终在小型GPU集群上实现了八面体纳米颗粒设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全原子生成蛋白质模型在联合建模多链复合物时，token对和原子对表示随链数和残基数呈二次增长，超出单GPU显存限制，阻碍了大型多聚体复合物的端到端设计。

Method: 提出了两种基于上下文并行（CP）的推理策略：1D行分片和2D网格分片结合环注意力，将二次激活分布到多GPU网格上，同时保持预训练权重不变；利用强点群对称性约束使CP可直接用于端到端全原子二十面体纳米颗粒设计。

Result: 在二十面体组装体采样中，最大可行不对称亚基（ASU）大小随GPU数量呈平方根增长趋势，2D分片策略在墙钟时间缩放上更优；所设计的二十面体纳米颗粒在结构和界面指标上表现良好；在16GB工作站级GPU小型集群上成功展示了八面体纳米颗粒设计。

Conclusion: Design-CP提供了一条实用路径，能够将大型组装体蛋白质设计民主化，降低硬件门槛，使在小型GPU集群上即可完成过去需要大规模算力的大型蛋白质复合物设计任务。

Abstract: Many all-atom generative protein models can in principle design large multimeric complexes by jointly modelling all chains, but their quadratic token- and atom-pair representations quickly exceed single-GPU memory as the number of chains and residues modelled grows. We introduce Design-CP, two context-parallel (CP) inference strategies for RFdiffusion 3 (1D row-sharding and 2D grid sharding with ring attention) that distribute the quadratic activations across a multi-GPU mesh while preserving pretrained weights. We characterise their scaling when sampling icosahedral assemblies, showing that the maximum feasible asymmetric subunit (ASU) size grows with the expected square-root trend in GPU count and that 2D sharding achieves better wall-clock scaling. Moreover, we show how strong point-group symmetry constraints make CP usable out of the box for end-to-end, all-atom design of icosahedral nanoparticles, yielding favourable in silico structural and interface metrics. Finally, we demonstrate octahedral nanoparticle design on a small cluster of workstation-grade 16GB GPUs, illustrating how Design-CP can be a practical path towards democratising large-assembly protein design.

</details>

*Weizhe Tang,Jiaxi Liu,Junwei you,Steven T. Parker,Pei Li,Sikai Chen,Meng Ran,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAIA框架，通过几何感知的距离去噪实现户外UWB工作区几何重建，在真实数据集上MSE降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外UWB测距易受非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差干扰，导致下游空间重建失真；现有方法缺乏对几何一致性的考虑。

Method: 提出GAIA框架，结合时间序列距离建模、潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影，将距离去噪作为监督任务，引导学习到的距离朝向边界一致的重建。

Result: 在真实户外UWB数据集上，GAIA取得最低的总体范围MSE和最高的多边形IoU，相比PoseMLP，MSE降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%。

Conclusion: 几何感知的距离去噪为实现空间一致的工作区重建提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate work-zone geometry perception is critical for intelligent transportation systems, and ultra-wideband sensing offers a low-cost approach for infrastructure-aided reconstruction. However, outdoor UWB ranging is often degraded by non-line-of-sight propagation, burst noise, and long-tail errors, which can distort downstream spatial reconstruction. We present GAIA, a geometry-aware, infrastructure-anchored learning framework that couples temporal range modeling with latent anchor-layout estimation and deterministic distance projection. GAIA preserves range denoising as the supervised task while orienting the learned distances toward boundary-consistent reconstruction. We evaluate GAIA on a real-world outdoor UWB dataset with synchronized UWB, GNSS, and IMU measurements, and further test robustness using a real-data-calibrated stress-test simulator. GAIA achieves the lowest overall range MSE and highest polygon IoU among evaluated filtering-based and learning-based baselines, reducing MSE by 18.4% and improving polygon IoU by 15.5% over PoseMLP. These results show that geometry-aware range denoising provides an effective path toward spatially coherent work-zone reconstruction.

</details>


### [75] [EquiFiLM: Charge-Conditioned Equivariant Force Fields via Feature-wise Linear Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05559)
*Samuel Sahel-Schackis,Ken-ichi Nomura,Aiichiro Nakano,Matthias F. Kling,Thomas Linker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EquiFiLM是一种轻量级扩展方法，通过在等变基础机器学习力场中逐层添加特征线性调制（FiLM）模块，实现对带电、外场等外部条件变化的连续调节，以少量数据即可高效模拟非平衡态物理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础机器学习力场（如MACE-MP-0、UMA）虽能覆盖广阔化学空间且接近DFT精度，但仅假设平衡基态物理，无法原生处理充电、外加电场、电子激发等外部诱导的电子态变化，限制了其在光激发、电荷注入等驱动过程中的应用。

Method: 提出EquiFiLM，为任何等变基础MLFF添加逐层特征线性调制（FiLM）块，仅调节标量通道，严格保持E(3)等变性，以最小训练数据学习外部驱动对势能面的影响。以MACE-MatPES为骨干网络，带电液态水为测试体系，得到E-MACE模型。

Result: 在四个训练电荷上，E-MACE相比未使用EquiFiLM的基线（相同训练数据），力RMSE降低3.1倍（21.3→6.96 meV/Å），每原子能量RMSE降低61倍（6.1→0.1 meV/atom），推理成本无差异。在七个插值与外推电荷上，力RMSE保持在18-61 meV/Å，能量RMSE在0.7-5.4 meV/atom。模型在整个测试范围内运行稳定的分子动力学，并预测了电荷依赖的约化对分布函数第一壳层响应。

Conclusion: EquiFiLM配方与骨干网络和条件变量无关，无需架构修改即可应用于任何含标量相互作用层通道的等变MLFF，仅需数千DFT标记帧（而非从头训练的约10^8结构）即可添加条件控制轴。

Abstract: Foundation machine learning force fields (MLFFs) such as MACE-MP-0 and UMA cover broad chemical space at near density functional theory (DFT) accuracy. However, they assume equilibrium ground-state physics and do not natively handle externally induced changes to the electronic state, such as charging, applied fields, or electronic excitation, which limits their use for driven processes such as photoexcitation and charge injection. We propose EquiFiLM, a lightweight extension that adds continuous external conditioning to any equivariant foundation MLFF via a per-layer Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) block, learning externally driven changes to the potential energy surface from minimal training data. The block modulates only scalar channels and preserves E(3)-equivariance exactly. We demonstrate the recipe on charged liquid water with the foundation model MACE-MatPES as the backbone, yielding E-MACE. On the four training charges, E-MACE delivers a $3.1\times$ reduction in force RMSE ($21.3$ to $6.96$ meV/$\mathring{A}$) and a $61\times$ reduction in per-atom energy RMSE ($6.1$ to $0.1$ meV/atom) over a baseline without EquiFiLM trained on the same data, at indistinguishable inference cost. Across seven held-out interpolation and extrapolation charges, force RMSE stays within $18-61$ meV/$\mathring{A}$ and energy RMSE within $0.7-5.4$ meV/atom. The model runs stable molecular dynamics across the full range tested and predicts the charge-dependent first-shell response of the reduced pair distribution function probed by ultrafast electron diffraction. Adding this conditioning axis to the foundation requires only a few thousand DFT-labeled frames, against the $\approx 10^8$ structures of a charge-aware foundation trained from scratch. The recipe is backbone- and conditioning-agnostic: it applies without architectural change to any equivariant MLFF with scalar interaction-layer channels.

</details>


### [76] [The Granularity Paradox: How Temporal Disaggregation Inflates In-Sample Fit and Compounds Out-of-Sample Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05450)
*Hugo Moreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列预测中的"粒度悖论"：更细的时间粒度（如周/日）虽能增加样本量并提升样本内拟合，但因递归误差累积导致样本外精度下降；粗粒度（如年）虽减少误差传播但数据量不足。通过对10个模型在6种粒度下的实证分析，验证了非单调阈值结构，并发现LSTM在日粒度下能克服误差惩罚，而线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定，指出标准点指标（RMSE/MAE）会系统性掩盖累积误差传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对时间序列预测中粒度选择与预测精度之间关系的系统性形式化分析，尤其是细粒度下样本量增加与递归误差累积之间的权衡机制尚未被充分理解。此外，标准评估指标无法揭示累积误差传播问题，导致模型评估产生误导。

Method: 形式化定义了粒度悖论中的权衡关系，并使用包含10种模型（涵盖朴素、统计、机器学习和深度学习架构）在6种时间粒度（年、半年、季、月、双周、日）上，基于13年公共采购数据集进行系统基准测试，引入一致性-分歧性诊断方法比较点指标与累积TPFE的方向行为。

Result: 递归自回归和季节模型在高频预测下显著退化（如Holt-Winters在日粒度下Test R²为-151，TPFE达425.85%）；LSTM呈现U形误差曲线，从月（19.66%）恶化到双周（35.94%），但在日粒度克服误差惩罚（TPFE 4.35%，R² 0.66）；线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定（TPFE 16.3%-17.0%），证实悖论由递归反馈拓扑驱动而非模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 粒度悖论真实存在且具有非单调阈值结构，标准点指标会系统性掩盖累积误差传播，应使用目标依赖的累积指标进行模型评估；线性回归的稳定性表明模型选择比单纯追求复杂度更重要；LSTM在极高频率下展现了克服递归误差累积的潜力。

Abstract: This paper explores the "Granularity Paradox" in time-series forecasting, wherein finer temporal disaggregation (e.g., Monthly to Weekly/Daily) improves in-sample diagnostics and dataset size (N), but degrades out-of-sample accuracy due to recursive error compounding over longer horizons (H). Conversely, coarse aggregation (Annual) eliminates recursive error propagation but reduces data available to estimators. We formalize this trade-off and benchmark 10 models - spanning naïve, statistical, machine learning, and deep learning architectures - across six granularities using a 13-year public procurement dataset. The empirical results reveal a non-monotonic threshold structure: recursive autoregressive and seasonal models degrade substantially under high-frequency forecasting (e.g., Holt-Winters reaches a Test R-squared of -151 and TPFE of 425.85% at the Daily grain), while the LSTM traces a U-shaped error curve, worsening from Monthly (19.66%) through Bi-Weekly (35.94%) before overcoming the error propagation penalty at Daily (TPFE of 4.35%, R-squared of 0.66). Linear Regression remains stable across all granularities (16.3-17.0% TPFE), confirming that the paradox is driven by recursive feedback topology, not model complexity. The results demonstrate that standard pointwise metrics (RMSE, MAE) systematically mask cumulative error propagation, and that evaluating forecasts without goal-dependent cumulative metrics produces misleading assessments of model adequacy. We introduce a consensus-dissensus diagnostic comparing the directional behaviour of pointwise metrics against cumulative TPFE across granularities, enabling the identification of models whose standard diagnostics mask systematic error propagation.

</details>


### [77] [Physics-Informed Neural Embeddings of PDE Solution Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06348)
*Raul Jimenez,Svitlana Mayboroda,Pavlos Protopapas,Leonid Sarieddine,David N. Spergel,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的框架，用于学习偏微分方程解族的有限维嵌入。该方法采用多头物理信息神经网络，共享体学习解空间的潜在流形，线性头重建不同初始条件下的解。头正交化惩罚消除潜在表示的简并性并稳定主成分谱。应用于一维粘性Burgers方程，发现潜在维度$n_b=20$时，仅2-4个主成分即可捕获约95%的潜在空间方差。还提出了傅里叶壳频率分析作为鲁棒的流形几何可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法学习PDE解空间嵌入时，缺乏物理一致性约束，且潜在表示存在简并性（多种等价的潜在编码对应同一解），导致结果不稳定、不可复现。此外，已有工作未系统量化解流形的有效维度及其在不同初始条件族下的变化。

Method: 提出多头物理信息神经网络（multihead PINN），共享网络体学习解空间的潜在流形，多个线性头分别对应不同初始条件的解。引入头正交化惩罚项消除潜在表示的旋转/置换简并性。利用傅里叶壳分解技术，将波数轴划分为频带，计算各频带对每个主成分的贡献量。

Result: 对一维粘性Burgers方程，潜在维度$n_b=20$时仅2-4个主成分捕获约95%方差，4-7个捕获约99%。热方程和波动方程验证了类似的维度压缩现象。傅里叶壳频率分析在不同训练运行间具有不变性。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了潜在谱和主成分作为解流形几何的鲁棒可观测量，正交化惩罚确保了表示的可复现性，傅里叶壳分析提供了跨训练鲁棒的流形几何刻画工具。

Abstract: We introduce a physics-informed framework for learning finite-dimensional embeddings of solution families of partial differential equations. The method uses a multihead Physics-Informed Neural Network in which a shared body learns a latent manifold representing the solution space, while linear heads reconstruct individual solutions associated with different initial conditions. A head-orthogonalization penalty removes degeneracies in the latent representation and stabilizes the principal-component spectrum across training realizations. Because the initial condition is built into the network output by construction, these principal components measure the additional variability the network learns on top of the initial profile, not the full solution itself. We apply the method to the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, with the heat and wave equations as robustness checks. For a latent dimension $n_b=20$, the learned manifolds exhibit pronounced effective dimensional reduction: for Burgers dynamics, only $2$-$4$ principal components capture about $95\%$ of the latent-space variance, while $4$-$7$ capture about $99\%$, depending on the initial-condition family; the same qualitative compression holds for the heat and wave equations. We also split the wavenumber axis into bands (``Fourier shells'') and measure how much each band contributes to every principal component. The resulting frequency profile is invariant under the change-of-basis freedom that the orthogonalization penalty leaves in the latent space, and is therefore reproducible across independent training runs. More broadly, this establishes the learned spectral profiles and principal components as robust observables of solution-manifold geometry.

</details>


### [78] [Exogenous Dropout: A Simple, Strong Baseline for Corruption-Robust Time Series Forecasting with Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05452)
*Hao Hu,Xue-shan Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出外生随机失活（exogenous dropout），一种在训练时随机置零外生通道的模型无关方法，能显著提升时间序列预测模型在外生协变量受噪声、时间错位或缺失时的鲁棒性，且不牺牲干净精度。实验表明该方法比专门设计的带界架构（BoundEx）更鲁棒，证明显式架构约束并非鲁棒性必需。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在部署时，外生协变量常出现噪声、时间错位或缺失等问题，导致依赖外生变量的模型性能严重下降，甚至低于仅用内生变量的基线。研究者希望探究这种鲁棒性是否需要专用架构，还是可以通过简单的训练干预实现。

Method: 提出外生随机失活（exogenous dropout），一种模型无关的训练方法，在训练阶段随机将整个外生通道置为零，迫使模型不过度依赖外生协变量。在电力价格预测、水库水文学和气象学三个领域进行实验，并与专门设计的带界架构BoundEx（含可学习门控、回退残差和外生FiLM调制）进行对比。

Result: 外生随机失活在高斯噪声、时间错位和完全缺失通道的场景下大幅提升鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据下的预测精度。应用于双关联网络时，该方法成为实验中最鲁棒的模型，在所有领域均优于BoundEx。架构-失活消融实验、门控行为诊断和表示级界限分析表明，显式的架构有界性并非鲁棒性必需。

Conclusion: 显式架构有界性并不是实现外生鲁棒性的必要条件，无界模型配合外生随机失活比专门设计的带界模型更加鲁棒。推荐外生随机失活作为未来时间序列外生协变量预测研究的简单且强大的基线方法，并发布了一个损坏鲁棒性基准测试集。

Abstract: Time series forecasters that use exogenous covariates are fragile in deployment: when those covariates are noised, temporally misaligned, or missing, strong exogenous-fusion and exogenous-adapted models can degrade far above the endogenous-only floor. We study whether such robustness requires specialized architectures, or whether it can be obtained through a simple training intervention. We propose exogenous dropout, a model-agnostic method that randomly zeros whole exogenous channels during training. Across electricity-price forecasting, reservoir hydrology, and meteorology, exogenous dropout substantially improves robustness under Gaussian noise, temporal misalignment, and fully missing channels, while preserving clean accuracy. Applied to a dual-correlation network, it yields the most robust model in our experiments, outperforming a deliberately strong bounded architectural foil, BoundEx, which combines a learnable gate, a fallback residual to the endogenous backbone, and per-channel exogenous FiLM modulation. Architecture-by-dropout ablations, gate-behavior diagnostics, and a representation-level bound show that explicit architectural boundedness is not necessary for this robustness: an unbounded model trained with exogenous dropout is more robust than the bounded model in every domain. We release a corruption-robustness benchmark and recommend exogenous dropout as a simple, strong baseline for future work on time series forecasting with covariates.

</details>


### [79] [Empirical Minimal-Realisation Compression of Deep Neural Networks via Controllability-Observability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05457)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可控性-可观性框架的深度神经网络状态阶数约简方法，通过构建数据驱动的可达性、可观性及平衡Gramian矩阵，计算层级的可达-可观秩，并将其作为压缩后的网络宽度。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，该方法在显著压缩状态和参数的同时，基本保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络存在大量隐藏状态冗余，但现有压缩方法（权重剪枝、量化等）未显式刻画内部状态的动力学角色。本文旨在从动力系统视角，利用可控性和可观性理论对网络隐藏状态进行系统性的阶数约简。

Method: 将训练好的DNN视为深度索引的非线性动力系统，从隐藏状态快照和输出Jacobian矩阵构造数据驱动的可达性、可观性及平衡Gramian矩阵，据此计算层级的可达秩、可观秩及联合可达-可观秩，并将其作为压缩后的层宽，构建约简网络。

Result: MNIST上4层SiLU DNN从1024阶约简至277阶，状态压缩72.95%，参数压缩73.48%，精度从96.60%降至95.45%；CIFAR-10上从4608阶约简至1339阶，状态压缩70.94%，参数压缩83.09%，精度基本保持不变（54.45%→54.44%），CUDA推理延迟降低约3倍。

Conclusion: 平衡可达-可观秩为设计紧凑神经网络提供了一种有原则的经验最小实现准则，能在几乎不损失精度的情况下显著压缩模型。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain substantial hidden-state redundancy, but most compression methods operate directly on weights, neurons, or quantised representations without explicitly characterising the dynamical role of internal states. This paper proposes a controllability-observability framework for empirical state-order reduction of deep neural networks. By viewing a trained network as a depth-indexed nonlinear dynamical system, we construct data-driven reachability, observability, and balanced Gramians from hidden-state snapshots and output Jacobians. The resulting A/B/C tests estimate layer-wise reachable, observable, and jointly reachable--observable ranks. These ranks are then used not only as diagnostic measures of hidden-state redundancy, but also as actual compressed layer widths for realised reduced networks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 compare the proposed balanced realisation against projection-based reduction, unstructured pruning, structured pruning, low-rank SVD, dynamic INT8 quantisation, and linear baselines. On MNIST, a four-layer SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 1024 to 277, giving 72.95% state compression and 73.48% parameter compression, while maintaining 95.45% accuracy compared with 96.60% for the full model. On CIFAR-10, a larger SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 4608 to 1339, giving 70.94% state compression and 83.09% parameter compression, while preserving accuracy from 54.45% to 54.44% and reducing CUDA inference latency by approximately 3X. The results show that balanced reachable-observable ranks provide a principled empirical minimal-realisation criterion for designing compact neural architectures with little or no loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [80] [Learning to Control LLM Agent Harnesses with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05458)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM agent的执行框架本身视为可学习的控制层，通过Harness MDP训练轻量级控制器来优化结构化执行动作，在冻结LLM的情况下提升验证行为和任务质量，并通过Harness Maturity Score区分过程行为与最终答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM agent的改进集中在提示词、模型或手工工作流上，而执行框架（harness）被视为固定基础设施。作者认为这一框架本身是可学习的控制层，值得优化。

Method: 将框架操作形式化为有限时域Harness MDP，由轻量级控制器选择结构化执行动作，LLM执行器保持冻结。控制器通过离线rollouts使用优势加权回归（仅终端任务评分奖励）训练。同时提出Harness Maturity Score（HMS）分离最终答案质量与过程行为评估。

Result: 在6个受控域和2个公共基准适配器上，学习到的控制器一致改善验证行为，并选择性提升最终任务质量，最大增益出现在tau-bench retail、AgentBench DB-Bench和带校准结构验证器的编码任务。消融实验排除模仿学习或简单添加检查的解释。

Conclusion: Harness控制可作为冻结LLM agent的可学习层，但离线数据支持限制了过程控制向最终答案质量的转化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are usually improved by changing prompts, models, or hand-written workflows, while the execution harness around the model is treated as fixed infrastructure. We argue that this harness is itself a learnable control layer. We formalize harness operation as a finite-horizon Harness MDP, where a lightweight controller selects structural execution actions while the LLM executor remains frozen. The controller is trained from offline rollouts using advantage-weighted regression with only terminal task-rubric rewards. We also separate final task quality from a post-hoc Harness Maturity Score, which measures whether the harness follows reliable execution patterns rather than only whether the final answer is correct. This separation gives a finite-buffer view of harness learning: final-quality gains require high-return support in the offline buffer, while process behavior can shift whenever it aligns with advantage-weighted actions. Across six controlled domains and two public-benchmark adapters, the learned controller consistently improves verification behavior and selectively improves final task quality, with the largest gains on adapted tau-bench retail, adapted AgentBench DB-Bench, and coding with a calibrated structural verifier. Ablations against behavior cloning and Forced CHECK show that the gains are not explained by imitation or by simply adding checks. These results identify harness control as a learnable layer for frozen LLM agents, while showing that offline support limits when better process control becomes better final answers.

</details>


### [81] [AdaStop: Cost-Aware Early Stopping for DNN Test Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05461)
*Bonan Shen,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaStop框架，通过将DNN测试建模为成本-收益决策过程，当估计的边际故障发现率低于阈值τ=c/v时自动停止标注，在仅使用9-31%标注预算的情况下发现65-84%的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法在固定标注预算下优先选择可能揭示故障的测试输入，但实际中难以选择合适的预算：预算过小会遗漏故障，预算过大会造成不必要的标注成本。因此需要研究测试过程中的停止问题。

Method: 将测试形式化为成本-收益决策过程，其中标注一个输入产生成本c，发现一个故障产生价值v。提出AdaStop框架，在测试过程中估计边际故障发现率，当估计率低于阈值τ=c/v时停止标注。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和选择策略上的实验表明，仅使用9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。

Conclusion: AdaStop能够有效解决DNN测试中的停止问题，在标注成本与故障发现之间取得良好平衡，大幅降低不必要的标注开销。

Abstract: Existing methods for testing deep neural networks (DNNs) primarily prioritize test inputs likely to reveal model faults under a fixed labeling budget. In practice, choosing that budget is difficult: too little testing misses failures, while too much incurs unnecessary labeling costs. This work studies the stopping problem in DNN testing. We formulate testing as a cost--benefit decision process in which labeling an input incurs cost $c$ and discovering a fault yields value $v$. Based on this formulation, we introduce \textit{AdaStop}, a framework that estimates the marginal fault discovery rate during testing and stops labeling when the estimated rate falls below the threshold $τ= c/v$. Experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and selection strategies show that $65$--$84\%$ of faults can be discovered using only $9$--$31\%$ of the labeling budget.

</details>


### [82] [Learnable Weighting of Intra-Attribute Distances for Categorical Data Clustering with Nominal and Ordinal Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05464)
*Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的图视角距离度量，同时保留名义型和有序型属性的内在差异与顺序信息，并设计了一个将距离权重学习与数据划分融合为单一范式的聚类算法，避免了两阶段方法的次优解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类数据聚类方法在计算相异度时，将名义型与有序型属性等同处理，忽略了有序属性值间的相对顺序信息；同时，名义型与有序型属性之间存在值得探索的相互依赖关系，现有方法未能有效利用。

Method: 从图视角出发，提出一种统一的名义型和有序型属性内部距离度量方法，保留有序值的顺序关系；设计将属性内部距离权重学习与数据对象划分整合为单一学习范式的聚类算法。

Result: 实验结果表明所提算法在聚类性能上优于现有对比方法。

Conclusion: 将属性距离权重学习与聚类划分联合优化可有效避免次优解，统一处理名义型与有序型属性的距离度量能提升分类数据聚类效果。

Abstract: The success of categorical data clustering generally much relies on the distance metric that measures the dissimilarity degree between two objects. However, most of the existing clustering methods treat the two categorical subtypes, i.e. nominal and ordinal attributes, in the same way when calculating the dissimilarity without considering the relative order information of the ordinal values. Moreover, there would exist interdependence among the nominal and ordinal attributes, which is worth exploring for indicating the dissimilarity. This paper will therefore study the intrinsic difference and connection of nominal and ordinal attribute values from a perspective akin to the graph. Accordingly, we propose a novel distance metric to measure the intra-attribute distances of nominal and ordinal attributes in a unified way, meanwhile preserving the order relationship among ordinal values. Subsequently, we propose a new clustering algorithm to make the learning of intra-attribute distance weights and partitions of data objects into a single learning paradigm rather than two separate steps, whereby circumventing a suboptimal solution. Experiments show the efficacy of the proposed algorithm in comparison with the existing counterparts.

</details>


### [83] [Breaking Structural Isolation: Scalable Graph Clustering via Community-Aware Sampling and Structural Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05469)
*Jingyun Zhang,Hao Peng,Jianxin Li,Angsheng Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCISE是一个可扩展的无监督图聚类框架，通过社区感知采样和约束结构熵协同解决图对比学习中的"结构隔离"问题，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图对比学习的无监督聚类方法在批训练过程中存在"结构隔离"问题，难以捕捉表征全局拓扑分布的凝聚性社区结构。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：1）结构熵社区约束算子(SECC)，在约束解空间中优化结构信息以减少社区碎片化；2）社区感知采样扩展机制(CSampE)，将目标节点社区上下文纳入采样批次以保持拓扑完整性；3）结构对比学习模块(StructCL)，基于批内结构相似性优化边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间学习表示。

Result: 在六个主流基准数据集上的实验表明，SCISE显著优于最先进算法；消融研究和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在真实大规模图上的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 协同社区感知采样与约束结构熵可有效缓解图聚类中的结构隔离问题，在大规模图上实现可扩展且鲁棒的无监督聚类。

Abstract: Unsupervised graph clustering is a fundamental technique for uncovering underlying semantic patterns in large-scale networks. Although Graph Contrastive Learning has demonstrated promising performance, existing methods often suffer from the "structural isolation" issue during mini-batch training, making it challenging to capture cohesive community structures that characterize the global topological distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SCISE, a Scalable unsupervised graph Clustering framework that preserves structural Integrity by synergizing community-aware sampling with constrained Structural Entropy. Specifically, we first introduce the Structural Entropy Community Constraint operator (SECC), which optimizes structural information within a constrained solution space to mitigate community fragmentation and enhance partition cohesion. Second, to prevent global information loss during batch training, we design a Community-Aware Sampling Expansion (CSampE) mechanism that incorporates the community context of target nodes into sampling batches, effectively breaking structural barriers and preserving topological integrity. Finally, we devise a Structural Contrastive Learning (StructCL) module that refines edge weights based on intra-batch structural similarity, guiding the encoder to learn representations in a higher-order structural space. Extensive experiments on six mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCISE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms, with ablation studies and robustness analyses further validating its effectiveness and reliability for real-world large-scale graphs.

</details>


### [84] [Parameter-Free Encoders Remain Viable for RDB Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05476)
*Linjie Xu,David Wipf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了关系数据库中缺失值预测的通用方法，比较了参数化编码器与免参数编码器的性能，并通过理论分析和实验证明免参数编码器在多数任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关系数据库中，每次面对新的预测任务都从头训练模型成本高昂，因此需要一种通用的冻结基础模型。当前关于编码器设计（参数化 vs 免参数化）存在争议，本文旨在澄清这一模糊性。

Method: 理论分析：在标签作为输入的情况下，分析关系数据库编码器的特性，证明可训练编码器参数的有效性存在局限。实验验证：在多个基准测试任务上比较免参数编码器与参数化编码器的性能。

Result: 免参数编码器在多数相关基准测试任务中仍能表现出强劲性能，与参数化编码器相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 更简单的免参数编码器足以在多数任务中实现强性能，无需RDB特定的预训练或可训练参数，为关系数据库的通用预测提供了更简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: Given a relational database (RDB) storing heterogeneous tabular information, how can we predict missing (or future) values in some target column of interest? As the space of potential targets is vast across enterprise settings, it is preferable to avoid learning a new model from scratch each time there is a new prediction task. Frozen foundation models based on RDB-specific encoders provide a viable solution, but ideal design remains an open question. On the one hand, it has recently been argued that certain parameter-free subgraph encoders combined with single-table foundation models can achieve near SOTA performance, with no RDB-specific pre-training required. Meanwhile, other contemporary studies advocate for parameterized encoders pre-trained to exploit observable labels for learning task-specific representations. To address this ambiguity, we analyze RDB encoder properties specifically when labels are present as inputs, proving limitations on the potential efficacy of trainable encoder parameters. As empirical validation, we demonstrate that considerably simpler parameter-free encoders are still capable of strong performance across many relevant benchmarking tasks.

</details>


### [85] [InvWeaver: Deductive Feedback for Invariant Synthesis in Interacting-Loop Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05478)
*Guangyuan Wu,Weining Cao,Zehui Tan,Yuan Yao,Hengfeng Wei,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InvWeaver是一个针对多循环程序的神经符号不变量合成框架，通过循环级抽象、义务引导推理和最弱前置条件细化来暴露循环间依赖关系并传播证明义务，在82个多循环基准中解决72个，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环不变量推断是程序验证中的基础挑战，现有LLM辅助方法在单循环程序上表现良好，但在处理包含多个交互循环的程序时往往困难重重。

Method: InvWeaver采用神经符号方法，核心思想是通过循环级抽象暴露循环间依赖关系，结合义务引导推理和最弱前置条件细化来传播证明义务，实现多循环程序的不变量合成。

Result: 在82个多循环基准问题中成功解决72个，同时在单循环任务上保持强性能，大幅超越现有不变量推断方法。

Conclusion: InvWeaver通过显式处理循环间交互，有效拓展了LLM辅助不变量推断方法到多循环程序场景，验证了神经符号方法在程序验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Loop invariant inference is a fundamental yet challenging problem in program verification. Recent LLM-aided guess-and-check techniques have shown strong performance on single-loop programs, but they often struggle with programs containing multiple interacting loops. This paper presents InvWeaver, a neuro-symbolic framework for synthesizing invariants for such programs. The key idea is to expose inter-loop dependencies and propagate proof obligations through a combination of loop-level abstraction, obligation-guided inference, and weakest-precondition-based refinement. We evaluate InvWeaver on a comprehensive benchmark suite, including a newly curated dataset derived from classic algorithms. Experimental results show that InvWeaver substantially outperforms existing invariant inference methods, solving 72 out of 82 multi-loop benchmark problems and maintaining strong performance on single-loop tasks.

</details>


### [86] [PatchOptic for Shared-State LLM Workflows with Projected Views and Verified Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05483)
*Zhaoyu Bai,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchOptic，一种基于光学（optics）理念的接口，用于在共享结构化状态的LLM工作流中实现本地更新的全局有效性验证。通过投影读取和结构化补丁验证，确保局部更新不会破坏全局状态一致性，并支持工作流步骤的委托、组合和静态重排序证书。在46个跨领域案例的PatchBench上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工作流依赖渐进式披露（progressive disclosure）来管理状态片段，但缺乏确保局部改写（patch）应用到完整状态后仍保持全局有效性的机制。需要一种契约来桥接局部更新与全局一致性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 借鉴光学（optics）中双向访问器的组合思想，提出PatchOptic接口：每个工作流步骤声明投影读取视图、授权写入区域和补丁来源区域。运行时强制执行这些声明，同时利用路径级足迹支持委托、子工作流组合和独立步骤的静态重排序证书。

Result: 在PatchBench（46个跨领域案例）上的实验表明：投影读取减少了信息泄漏和token开销，同时在高性能模型（strong actor）下保持输出质量；运行时验证在提交前拦截契约违规；补丁读取验证拒绝使用隐藏来源的受损补丁。

Conclusion: PatchOptic通过声明式的工作流步骤契约，有效解决了共享状态LLM工作流中局部更新与全局有效性之间的冲突，为构建可靠的多步骤代理工作流提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: Agentic workflows often operate over shared, structured state. Because LLM context windows are limited, each model invocation is typically shown only the state fragment needed for the current workflow step, a pattern commonly known as progressive disclosure. Modern systems construct such model-facing views using grep-like keyword search, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), abstract-syntax-tree (AST) queries, and task-specific agent skills. These methods make the read side manageable, but they do not define when a locally proposed rewrite is valid after it is applied back to the full state. The missing piece is a contract between local updates and global validity. We introduce PatchOptic, an optic-inspired interface for shared-state LLM workflows. Optics are compositional bidirectional accessors that describe how views of structured data are read and updated. PatchOptic borrows this view/update intuition and realizes it through projected reads and verified structured patches. Each workflow step declares a projected read view, an authorized write region, and a patch-source region. Beyond runtime enforcement, the same declaration yields a path-level footprint that supports delegation, sub-workflow composition, and static certificates for reordering independent steps within the same phase. We evaluate this design with PatchBench, a benchmark with 46 cases across domains. The results show that projected reads reduce reported leakage and token cost while preserving accepted-output quality under the strong actor. Runtime verification blocks declared workflow-contract violations before commit, and patch-read enforcement rejects compromised patch artifacts that use hidden sources.

</details>


### [87] [$\mathbfλ$-VAE: Variance Equalization for Posterior Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05531)
*Girum Demisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并形式化了VAE后验坍塌的两个耦合原因（梯度失衡和信息鸿沟），提出通过修改重参数化步骤的λ-VAE方法，利用方差均衡机制解决该问题，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE经常出现后验坍塌问题，即近似后验收敛到先验，导致隐编码无信息。尽管已有大量研究，但对该问题为何发生的统一解释仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出λ-VAE，通过修改重参数化步骤：按每维指数缩放采样噪声，同时保持KL惩罚项使用原始后验方差。这种不对称性使稳定训练吸引子远离退化坍塌状态，驱动所有潜在维度朝向相同均衡（方差均衡机制）。从净信息增益目标导出每个维度的闭式最优指数，单个超参数控制重建-生成权衡。

Result: 在Binary MNIST、Binary Omniglot、CIFAR-10、CelebA-64基准上，一致减少了坍塌维度，信息容量提升高达2.8倍nats，重建质量提升高达+0.33 BPD。

Conclusion: λ-VAE通过单一修改解决了后验坍塌的两个耦合原因，方差均衡机制使所有隐维度趋向相同均衡，有效提升了VAE的信息容量和重建质量。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) frequently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which the approximate posterior converges to the prior, rendering the latent code uninformative. Despite extensive research, a unified account of why collapse occurs has remained an open question. We identify and formalize two logically independent but coupled causes. \emph{Gradient imbalance} occurs when the decoder's reconstruction signal vanishes faster than the $\mathbb{KL}$ regularization pressure as the posterior widens. \emph{Information gap} occurs when the stochastic sampling step discards a substantial fraction of the encoder's computed representation, attenuating decoder sensitivity and making collapse inexpensive. Both causes share the same collapse trajectory, and we show that the information gap is algebraically equivalent to mismatch between the aggregate posterior and the prior, unifying two pathologies. Subsequently, we introduce $λ$-VAE, which resolves both causes through a single modification to the reparameterization step: the sampling noise is scaled by per-dimension exponent, while the $\mathbb{KL}$ penalty retains the original posterior variance. This asymmetry shifts the stable training attractor away from the degenerate collapsed state, driving all latent dimensions toward the same equilibrium -- a mechanism we term \emph{variance equalization}. A closed-form optimal exponent per dimension follows from a net information gain objective, with a single hyperparameter controlling the reconstruction-generation tradeoff. We validate on standard benchmarks (Binary MNIST, Binary Omniglot, CIFAR-10, CelebA-64), showing consistent reductions in collapsed dimensions, information capacity gains of up to $2.8\times$ nats, and reconstruction quality improvements of up to $+0.33$ BPD.

</details>


### [88] [Self-Review Reinforcement Learning (SRRL) with Cross-Episode Memory and Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05541)
*Muhammad Zain Amin,Kibele Sebnem Yildirim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自我审查强化学习(SRRL)框架，通过在RL episode中嵌入明确的自我审查步骤，让模型在首次回答失败时生成自我审查以识别错误，从而改进第二次尝试。结合策略梯度优化和选择性蒸馏，以及跨episode记忆机制，在GSM8K基准上相比标准RLVR基线取得更优的最终奖励和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练大语言模型时，环境反馈通常稀疏或延迟，模型难以准确定位推理过程中哪些动作导致成功或失败，从而难以有效学习。

Method: 提出SRRL框架：(1) 在RL episode中嵌入显式自我审查步骤，首轮回答失败时生成自我审查分析错误原因，并据此条件化改进的第二次尝试；(2) 使用策略梯度优化自我审查；(3) 通过选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中；(4) 跨episode记忆机制保留成功自我审查供未来相似任务复用。

Result: 在Qwen 3-4B和OLMo-3-7B两个模型上使用GRPO优化器，在GSM8K基准上，SRRL始终优于标准RLVR基线，获得更高的最终奖励和更好的学习效率。

Conclusion: SRRL能够成功将环境反馈转化为行为改进，相比推理时刻的反思方法（如Reflexion），SRRL通过策略梯度优化和蒸馏将改进持久化到未来episode中，有效提升了学习效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is commonly used to train large language models using environmental feedback. In applied settings, the environment usually provides sparse or delayed feedback. This makes it difficult for the model to pinpoint which actions in its reasoning led to success or failure. So, learning effectively from these signals is hard because the model must determine how each failure should inform meaningful behavioral corrections in subsequent iterations. We introduce a training framework, Self-Review Reinforcement Learning, that embeds an explicit self-review step into each RL episode. When a first-pass response fails, the model generates a self-review to identify what went wrong, which conditions an improved second attempt. Unlike inference-time reflection approaches, such as Reflexion, the framework optimizes self-review with policy gradients and internalizes improvements into the base policy via selective distillation, ensuring they persist across future episodes. A cross-episode memory keeps successful self-reviews for reuse when encountering similar tasks in future episodes during training. We evaluate SRRL against a standard RLVR baseline using the GRPO optimizer across two language models, Qwen 3-4B and OLMo-3- 7B, on GSM8K benchmark. SRRL consistently outperforms the RLVR in final reward performance and achieves greater learning efficiency by successfully transforming feedback into behavioral improvement.

</details>


### [89] [Federated Physics-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Distributed Stability Control in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05553)
*Omar Al-Refai,Ibrahim Shahbaz,Adam Ali Husseinat,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPPO-PG，一种基于物理邻域信息的多智能体联邦近端策略优化框架，用于智能电网暂态稳定控制。将问题建模为合作多智能体强化学习，每个发电机只需获取两个最强电气耦合邻居的频率偏差，通过引导策略初始化和CTDE范式训练。在IEEE 39节点系统上，实现100%稳定率，平均稳定时间降低72.4%，控制功率降低7-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网中暂态稳定控制需要快速抑制故障后发电机频率和转子角偏差以防止级联故障，现有方法在去中心化执行与稳定性能之间存在权衡。

Method: 将暂态稳定控制建模为合作多智能体强化学习问题，使用联邦多智能体PPO算法；每个智能体利用Kron简化电纳矩阵识别两个最强耦合电气邻居的频率偏差；采用引导策略初始化（从经典去中心化控制器热启动）和CTDE架构中的集中式评论家。

Result: 在IEEE 39节点系统上，跨5个训练和3个未见故障场景，24次试验均实现100%稳定；平均稳定时间降低72.4%；控制功率降低7-14倍；推理延迟满足IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018实时报告要求。

Conclusion: FedPPO-PG在无需中央协调器的情况下实现了去中心化执行，同时显著优于集中式基线，满足实时性要求，为智能电网暂态稳定控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Transient stability control in smart grids requires rapid post-fault damping of generator frequency and rotor angle deviations to prevent cascading failures. This paper proposes FedPPO-PG, a Federated Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization framework with Physics-Grounded neighborhoods, which reformulates transient stability control as a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning problem optimized directly against closed-loop stability objectives. Each generator hosts an independent local actor augmented with the frequency deviations of its two most strongly coupled electrical neighbors, identified from the post-fault Kron-reduced susceptance matrix. A guided policy initialization phase warm-starts all actors from the classical decentralized controller, while a centralized critic guides advantage estimation under the centralized training--decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm. Evaluated on a simulation of the IEEE 39-bus benchmark system across five training and three unseen fault contingencies, FedPPO-PG achieves 100% stabilization in all 24 trials, reduces mean stability time by 72.4%, and cuts the control power by 7-14 times compared to the centralized baseline. Each actor executes independently with no central coordinator at deployment, and the per-actor inference latency satisfies the IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018 real-time reporting requirements.

</details>


### [90] [SafeImpute: Reliable Clinical Data Imputation via Conformal Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05613)
*Xinrui He,Mengting Ai,Junting Wang,Curtiss B. Cook,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeImpute 提出了一种面向不规则稀疏临床纵向记录的可信插补框架，通过事件图构建、双关系图神经网络与自适应融合进行插补学习，并利用共形p值与Benjamini-Hochberg过程控制插补结果中不可接受错误的假发现率（FDR），在保障准确性的同时实现统计可控的可靠结果释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中关键实验室指标常因就诊稀疏和检测不规律而大量缺失，现有插补方法虽能提升平均精度，但无法为高风险下游任务提供哪些插补值可靠、哪些不可靠的指导，亟需一种兼具准确性与统计可控可靠性的插补框架。

Method: 构建事件图以捕获患者内时间轨迹与患者间临床相似性；采用双关系图神经网络和自适应融合学习插补，辅以掩码重建目标进行正则化；将代理风险分数转化为共形p值，并应用Benjamini-Hochberg过程控制释放插补中不可接受错误的FDR。

Result: 在Mayo Clinic数据、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，SafeImpute在标准插补评估和FDR控制的选择性释放评估中均优于多种基线方法，实现了强插补精度与可靠的错误控制。

Conclusion: SafeImpute有效解决了稀疏不规则临床数据中插补值可靠性量化的问题，为高风险临床决策提供了统计上有保障的可信插补机制。

Abstract: Clinical care often relies on key laboratory indicators, yet real-world patient visits are sparse and tests are ordered irregularly, leading to pervasive missingness. While many imputation methods improve average accuracy, they provide limited guidance on which imputed values are reliable enough for high-stakes downstream use. In this work, we study reliable clinical imputation, aiming to produce accurate imputations while selectively releasing the reliable results, with statistical control over clinically unacceptable errors. To achieve this goal, we propose SafeImpute, a reliable imputation framework for irregular and sparse clinical longitudinal records. SafeImpute constructs an event graph that captures both intra-patient temporal trajectories and inter-patient clinical similarity, and learns imputations with a two-relation GNN and adaptive fusion, regularized by an auxiliary masked reconstruction objective. For reliability guarantees, SafeImpute converts a proxy risk score into conformal p-values and applies the Benjamini--Hochberg procedure to control the false discovery rate (FDR) of unacceptable errors among released imputations at a user-specified tolerance. Experiments on our Mayo Clinic data, the public MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets show that SafeImpute achieves strong imputation accuracy while providing reliable error control, outperforming diverse baselines in both standard imputation evaluation and FDR-controlled selective-release evaluation.

</details>


### [91] [A Coin Flip Per Token: Bernoulli Sparse Steering of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05615)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Yuqing Huang,Sagarika Suresh,Leizer Teran,Jithin Pradeep,Xiaotong Xu,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机令牌引导（STS）和随机块引导（SBS）两种稀疏干预方法，在保持流畅性的同时，仅用50%的令牌即可恢复大部分密集引导效果，并发现SAE介导的控制受累积信号剂量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导方法在每生成一个令牌时都施加扰动，导致流畅性下降。本文探究是否需要进行密集干预，即是否可以通过稀疏干预实现同样效果。

Method: 提出STS（每个令牌独立以概率p进行引导）和SBS（每个序列仅在引导窗口内进行一次引导），无需奖励模型或学习策略；在两个模型家族和两个行为任务上评估不同干预比例的效果。

Result: 引导50%的令牌即可恢复大部分密集引导效果并保持流畅性；引导30%的令牌即超过提示控制；最优引导强度与干预比例呈反比，表明SAE介导的控制受累积信号剂量限制。

Conclusion: 密集干预并非必要，稀疏令牌引导可在保持流畅性的同时高效实现行为控制，且SAE控制效果取决于序列中累积的干预信号总剂量。
